# Every skill of the game as the bot knows it (its L2DB.dat, the skill section), one line per skill ID.
# Made from the bot database; skills_export.txt reads it to fill skills.json with the skills of a character.
# id|oper|magic|range|name|lvl:range,...|icon - oper: 0 active, 1 active with a duration (buff / aura),
# 2 passive, 3 toggle; magic 1 = a magic skill; range = cast range at its lowest level (-1 / -2 = no target: on
# us / round us); lvl:range = from this skill level on the range is this (levels 101+ are enchant routes);
# icon = its line in skillicons.txt (from 0; a 16x16 PNG in base64, the bot L2ICO.dat), empty = none.
# The AoE radius is not here: the client does not have it (the server does) - set it in nav_view.html > Skills.
